#include <litop>
#include <out>
#include <cstring>

class example:
	public u64 size;
	public u32 revision;
	public u64 members;
	public u64 superclasses;
	public u8 type;

char* operator+(char* a, char* b);

void fsRead(char* path);

typecast char* its(u64 x):
	char* nstr = UTHS(x);
	return nstr;

typecast char* its(u32 x):
	(rdi) u64 x64;
	char* nstr = UTHS(x64);
	return nstr;

typecast char* its(u16 x):
	(rdi) u64 x64;
	char* nstr = UTHS(x64);
	return nstr;

typecast char* its(u8 x):
	(rdi) u64 x64;
	char* nstr = UTHS(x64);
	return nstr;

inline SYMBOL-testFunc_c2 u64 testFunc():
	return 4KiB;

void extest() {
	throw 0x500;
}

void main() {
	example ex;
	ex.size = 0x500;
	ex.revision = 2;
	ex.members = 18;
	ex.superclasses = 0x18;
	ex.type = 0x8E;
	(r13) u64 i1 = 0x800;
	(r14) u64 i2 = 0x100;
	async (i1,i2) {
		(r13) u64 o1 = i1+i2;
		(r14) char* str = `Thread reporting for duty ${o1}.`;
		out << str << endl;
	}
	try
	{
		u64 TI = 0x300;
		char* HW = `Hello ${TI+(0x200+0x400)+testFunc()} world.`;
		out << HW << endl;
		out << "ex: ";
		out << char*(ex) << endl;
	}
	catch(u64 e)
	{
		char* HW = `exception u64: ${e}`;
		out << HW << endl;
	}
	catch(u32 e)
	{
		char* HW = `exception u32: ${e}`;
		out << HW << endl;
	}
	out << "end\n";
}
